- **cascade**: Run tools/find_cascades.py to predict tier-promotion cascades from renames. With no args, prints the top-20 highest-cascade placeholders; with `&lt;module&gt; &lt;addr&gt;`, prints the cascade for that specific symbol. ([cntrl-alt-lenny/gx-spirit-caller](https://github.com/cntrl-alt-lenny/gx-spirit-caller/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cascade.md) · ★4)
- **cascade**: Run a task through the model cascade — fable analyses, opus plans, sonnet executes, haiku assists, with an upward escalation ladder. Token-minimal .cf handoffs; humans read one line per stage. ([jasonkneen/cascade](https://github.com/jasonkneen/cascade/blob/HEAD/skills/cascade/assets/claude-code/commands/cascade.md) · ★3)
- **cascade**: Analyze cascade change log and produce a structured implementation record with traceability. Use when user wants to document what was built, create a handoff record, or trace implementation to planning documents. ([NguyenNhan1610/ai-companion](https://github.com/NguyenNhan1610/ai-companion/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai/commands/cascade.md))
- **cascade-budget**: Compute the full link budget as a cascaded gain/loss train and report the margin at every stage. This is ray-tracing the optical system: light (signal power) enters, each surface adds gain or subtracts loss, and you read the throughput at the detector (the demodulator). ([DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab](https://github.com/DaxxSec/cognitropy-lab/blob/HEAD/satellite-communication-protocols/.claude/commands/cascade-budget.md) · ★1)
- **cascade-check**: Before modifying a type, interface, or shared export, assess the cascade risk. ([NellInc/MillOS](https://github.com/NellInc/MillOS/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cascade-check.md) · ★8)
- **cascade-check**: Ejecuta un cascade-closure check (paper §6.4) — invoca el subagent spec-guardian y sintetiza su output con acciones concretas. Usar antes de cortar un release, tras mergear un PR grande, o cuando sospechás drift. ([grodriguez1983/vairix-recruitment-platform](https://github.com/grodriguez1983/vairix-recruitment-platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/cascade-check.md))

- **ceo-loop**: Activate the CEO Decision Loop in ORBIT mode. Starts a continuous multi-iteration governance cycle: OBSERVE pending decisions → REASON with precedent → BUILD decisions → INSPECT retrograde → TURN for next iteration. The Stop hook keeps the loop alive until the queue is empty or limits are reached. Usage: /forge:ceo-loop [max-iterations] [time-limit-minutes] ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/nxtg-forge/commands/ceo-loop.md) · ★6)
- **ceo-loop-cancel**: Cancel the active CEO Decision Loop gracefully. Sets the loop to inactive, writes a final session summary to the progress file, and logs session statistics. ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/nxtg-forge/commands/ceo-loop-cancel.md) · ★6)
- **ceo-loop-cancel**: &gt; Gracefully stop the active CEO Decision Loop, write a final session summary with statistics, and preserve the complete decision journal for future sessions. ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/ceo-loop-cancel.md) · ★6)
